```
• Laptop · 7.6 GB

• Router itself · 0.5 GB
[ ✏️ Name a device ]                   (R50)
 -> ✏️ Which device do you want to name?
    [ Unknown device (…6e:94) ]
    [ iPhone ] [ Galaxy-S21 ]
    [ LG-TV ]  [ Cancel ]
    -> ✏️ Naming Unknown device (…6e:94)           (the same message, no buttons)
       ✏️ Send the new name for Unknown device (…6e:94) as a reply to this message.
       Up to 24 characters. Send - to remove a name.            (reply request, answer = the reply)
       -> ✅ Unknown device (…6e:94) is now called Kate's iPhone.
       -> ✅ Name removed: Kate's iPhone.
       -> ⚠️ That name is empty. Reply to the message above with a name of up to 24 characters.
    popup on an old button: This button expired, tap ✏️ Name a device again.
    reply to an old prompt: This request expired, tap ✏️ Name a device again.

⚠️ Restart the router?
Internet and Wi-Fi go down for about 2 min.
[ 🔄 Restart ]   [ Cancel ]
 -> 🔄 Restarting now (Sam tapped Restart).
 -> Restart cancelled.
 -> Restart not confirmed, nothing done.        (after 60 s)
 popup on an old button: This button expired, send /reboot again

🟢 Router back after restart (asked by Sam), down 1 min 50 s.

SMS replies (ASCII, one SMS each):
Router: internet OK (4G), signal fair. On since 7 Nov 11:01. Balance 0.31 EUR (7 Nov).
Router: internet DOWN since 14:20 (32 min). Self-repair step 3 of 6, cause: modem not responding. 2G backup: off.
Router: restarting now, back in about 2 min.
Router: reboot not done, it was already waiting when the router started. Send it again.
Router: reboot not done, the SMS is older than 10 min or has no time. Send it again.
Router: reboot not done, 5 restarts by SMS in the last day. Try again later.
Router commands: status, reboot

✉️ +15550100123 · today 11:11
> This is a test SMS.

✉️ Telekom 1466 · today 11:05
> Imate: 0.31 do 05.01.;
= Balance 0.31 EUR, valid until 5 Jan

💶 Asked Telekom for the balance (SMS to 1466). The answer comes here, usually within a minute.
 -> 💶 Balance: 0.31 EUR, valid until 5 Jan       (the same message, edited when the reply comes, R36)
    ⚠️ Top up at least 7.50 EUR, or the data package will not renew.
    > Telekom 1466, today 10:00:
    > Imate: 0.31 do 05.01.;

When one SMS in the modem cannot be read (R34h):
⚠️ One SMS in the modem cannot be read.
New SMS are not posted here and SMS commands do not work until it is deleted.
Delete it in LuCI: Modem, SMS.

📥 Newest 3 of 27 SMS
(each SMS as above, separated by an empty line)

❓ What the buttons do
🟢 Status – is the internet OK
📶 Wi-Fi – name, password and a QR code for guests
📱 Devices – who is connected, data this month, names
⚡ Speed – measure the speed (~60 s)
💶 Balance – ask Telekom (~1 min)          (operator name from the preset, R47; "not available for this SIM" without one, R47a)
✉️ SMS – newest SMS (/sms 10 for more)
🔔 Alerts – automatic messages, on or off
🔄 Reboot – restart the router (asks first)

The bot also posts by itself: new SMS, low balance, internet back, 2G backup.       (low balance only with a preset and `LOW_BALANCE`)

Once after the first start of the image: after a reset or a flash first a message of its own (R37f), then the greeting with the keyboard (R37e):
🔄 Router was reset to its built-in settings.
Earlier logs and saved SMS are gone.

🤖 The router bot is ready. Tap a button below, or send /help to see what each one does.

Keyboard under the message field (R37e), persistent, resized:
[ 🟢 Status ] [ 📶 Wi-Fi ]  [ 📱 Devices ]
[ ⚡ Speed ]  [ 💶 Balance ] [ ✉️ SMS ]
[ 🔔 Alerts ] [ 🔄 Reboot ] [ ❓ Help ]

In a tailscale image (R69c), 10 buttons in 3 rows:
[ 🟢 Status ] [ 📶 Wi-Fi ] [ 📱 Devices ] [ ⚡ Speed ]
[ 💶 Balance ] [ ✉️ SMS ] [ 🔔 Alerts ]
[ 🔒 Tailscale ] [ 🔄 Reboot ] [ ❓ Help ]
```

The "Enough for" line of a speed test result by verdict: `fast` ✅ 4K video on several devices and video calls; `OK` ✅ HD video and video calls; `slow` ⚠️ browsing and low-quality video; `very slow` ❌ messages only.

## Decisions

| ID | Decision | Why | Date |
|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | Official ImageBuilder 25.12.6 instead of a third-party build such as eko.one.pl (R1) | a stable release and a reproducible build | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D2 | The configuration lives in the image (R7, R31, R31a) | a button reset must bring back a working router, not bare OpenWrt | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D3 | LTE only, automatic mode rejected (R10, R11) | a modem that falls back to EDGE does not return to LTE by itself | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D4 | Band lock per profile (R11c) | a modem may move from a strong band to one that is weak at the site, and data then stalls | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D5 | AT instead of QMI in the scripts (R12a, R12c), with one exception, D80 | killed `uqmi` calls used up the modem's QMI client IDs and blocked bringing `wwan` up | 2026-11-11 (from: commit 5af53c522b) |
| D6 | Watchcat plus the project's own `wwan-recover` (R14, R15) | watchcat cannot grow the gaps between its actions, so the script only does the router restart | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D7 | A ladder of growing gaps (R16) | short failures get a quick restart, and a long one does not restart the router all day | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D8 | A limit of 6 restarts per failure (R19) | a used-up account must not drive the router into restarts for weeks | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D9 | A cycle counter instead of time (R21, R21e) | the router has no RTC, and after a restart without NTP the clock goes back | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D10 | The failure class never holds a restart back (R18a) | the modem can report a connection while data is stuck | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D11 | No step between the `wwan` restart and the router restart (R16) | `AT+CFUN=1,1` and a USB unbind restart the whole board anyway | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D12 | The EDGE fallback ends at a restart (R24) | a person who restarts the router gets plain LTE back, and the EDGE rules decide again | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D13 | The generic image has no secrets and no data of a private profile (R4, R4a) | the generic image is meant to be shared; a built image carries its WiFi password and root hash, so the repository is shared, not a built image, and each router gets its own build | 2026-11-09 (from: commit 09426ce17a) |
| D14 | Packages modemdata and modemband (R2, R30a) | operators can lock the modem onto one band, so LuCI must show and set the bands and the signal | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D15 | A private image holds its profile's secrets and stays out of git (R5, R33b) | the image is ready to flash with no further steps, and the repository never holds secrets | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D16 | Automatic SMS cleaning (R26) | a full SMS store stops delivery | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D17 | Signal log (R28) | a record of the signal makes link failures diagnosable afterwards | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D19 | HTTPS for LuCI (R30) | the root password does not cross the WiFi in clear text | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D20 | The profile is a file of variables; `generic.conf` is in git, other profiles are ignored (R3, R5) | one parser checks every key, and private profiles stay local | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D21 | Defaults of the generic image and random passwords (R7a, R33) | a fixed password in a public repository is known to everyone who reads it | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D22 | Taking over a dead `wwan-recover` lock (R21a) | without it one killed process turns the repair off until the next restart | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D23 | The generic image does not force bands (R11c) | a band mask fits only one site | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D24 | No EDGE when the package is used up (R25) | after a top-up the router should be on LTE at once | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D25 | WireGuard disabled in the generic image (R7a, R32) | the template knows no server to connect to | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D26 | Package wifischedule (R2, R30a) | the WiFi can be switched off on a schedule from LuCI | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D27 | APN from the profile, otherwise empty, and the operator table only as fallback (R13, R13a, R13b) | the APN is not stored on the SIM, and an empty APN lets most networks assign their own | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D28 | Bands and IMSI through the project's own AT commands on the scripts' port (R12) | `modemband.sh` and a fallback IMSI read used the LuCI port and bypassed the lock | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D29 | The wait for the lock counts toward the command's limit (R12b, R13c) | the APN script runs while the link comes up, and a hotplug script stuck on an AT command can take the router down | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D30 | EDGE after the first failed restart, a failure ends only on LTE, LTE probes only after the limit (R15, R16, R22, R23, R23a) | a failure that a restart did not fix is a failure of the LTE network, not of the modem, so waiting for the sixth restart before EDGE means almost a day without access | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D31 | Class `account` from an operator DNS answer, only for cycles in LTE-only mode, limit class saved in the state (R18, R18b, R18c) | with a used-up package the operator DNS answers, while "nothing comes back" depended on whether someone was using the WiFi | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D32 | EDGE comes back after a start in passive mode (R22a) | without it a power cut or a manual restart during a long failure leaves the router without access until LTE returns | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D33 | The snapshot does not wait for a busy AT lock (R20a) | the restart must not depend on the modem that is suspected of hanging | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D34 | APN fallback from the second failed attempt, with the "from table" marker, in the background (R13b, R13d) | the first attempt with an empty APN fails also on networks that accept it, and a script blocking in hotplug can hang the router | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D35 | Code, profiles and images in this folder, the ImageBuilder in the work folder (R3, R5) | the sources stay small and versioned, and the heavy downloads stay out of the repository | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D36 | A password card with hints, passwords repeated between builds (R33, R33a) | after a reset and after a later image the router still has the passwords of the card that came with it, and its behaviour is explained there, not in syslog | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D37 | Image identity and a first-start entry (R7b) | a reset erases logs and state, so without it a router after a reset cannot be told from one without a failure, nor can the image version be known | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D38 | Class `sim` for a SIM without `READY` (R18, R18d) | a restart does not enter a PIN, and a SIM with a PIN otherwise gives a day of restarts without a message | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D39 | A fixed format of the failure state and of the end of `wwan-recover` entries (R21d, R21e) | a person or a tool reads the course of a failure without guessing, also when the clock went back | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D40 | Class order `sim`, `account`, `hang`, with `sim` and `account` kept to the end of the failure (R18) | watchcat bringing `wwan` up while the package is used up gave random `hang` classes that opened EDGE against D24 | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D41 | The LTE probe after the limit runs as normal cycles in LTE-only mode, its class replacing the limit class (R18b, R23) | otherwise a package that runs out after the limit never gives the class `account`, and the router goes back to EDGE | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D42 | Logs of the image's scripts in English (R29b) | log lines are read by people and tools in any language, and ASCII shows correctly everywhere | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D43 | SMS forwarding to Telegram, turned on by profile keys (R34, R3, R38a) | the owner sees the operator's SMS without logging in to the router; the generic image has no bot | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D44 | One reminder when the balance drops below the profile's threshold, without recognising packages (R35, R36) | packages differ, and an account may be left empty on purpose | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D45 | A two-way bot with English commands that only read (R37, R37a) | the token sits in the image, so a taken-over bot must not be able to change anything | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D46 | Polling the store every 15 s in a resident service instead of listening for `+CMTI` (R34, R38) | forwarding within seconds, and a constant reader on the AT port without `sms_tool` hung the modem | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D47 | The automatic balance query as the only SMS the image sends by itself (R27, R35) | the balance cannot be read any other way, and one query a week keeps the cost low | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D48 | The bot can live in a group (R37b, R37) | membership of the group decides who may use the bot, with no list in the image | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D49 | Messages for a non-technical person, technical data in one line at the end of `/status` (R39, R39a, R39b, R39c, R39d, R39e) | not everyone in the chat is technical, and the numbers stay available for diagnosis | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D50 | A message when the internet comes back after a failure longer than one repair cycle (R40) | nothing can be said during the failure, and short drops give no message | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D51 | The last outage in `/status`, with an approximate time when NTP is missing (R40a, R40b) | the router has no RTC (D9), and an approximate time is better than none | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D52 | A message when switching to 2G, once per failure (R40c) | Telegram works on 2G, so the owner can learn about a failure while it lasts | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D53 | A switch for the 2G message in the bot (R37, R37a, R37c) | the message is useful to some and noise to others; the switch does not touch the link | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D54 | `/speedtest` from the bot through `speedtestcpp` and speedtest.net servers (R41, R37a, R2) | a measurement of its own through `uclient-fetch` stalled on TLS and broke uploads; the larger image is accepted | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D55 | No confirmation for the test, and no data warning in the start reply (R41) | one tap is enough, and a test uses a few hundred MB, small next to a typical package | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D56 | Previous and last result only from today, kept in RAM (R41b) | an older result says little about the link now | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D57 | The "offline" alarm comes from healthchecks.io as a webhook through the bot, after 30 min of silence, once, without "up" (R42, R42a, R3) | the router cannot reach Telegram during a failure; the alarm must not spam, and its short text guesses no cause, since a planned power-off is silent too | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D58 | `/wifi` with name and password (R43) | the guest password can be passed on from a phone | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D59 | `/devices` and the month's data from `nlbwmon`, with its database on flash (R44, R44a) | a database in RAM was zeroed at every restart and, after a start without NTP, landed in the wrong month | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D60 | `/reboot` and the `/alerts` switch with Telegram buttons (R45, R37d, R37a) | one tap to confirm instead of typing | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D61 | SMS control with `status`, `reboot`, `help` from the profile's numbers, without confirmation, with a limit of 10 replies per day (R46, R46a, R46b, R46c, R46d, R27, R3) | it is the only channel when data does not work; the limit protects the balance and stops a loop with an automatic responder | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D62 | No band unlock during a failure | the LTE band mask does not apply to EDGE, and unlocking a band the profile excluded can stall data, so it would spoil the LTE probes after the limit | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D63 | `/devices` also shows devices not connected now, with their remembered name (R44, R44b) | a device switched on only in the evening otherwise vanished from the list, its data showing only in the sum | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D64 | A persistent bot keyboard, with the bot's privacy mode on (R37e) | a non-technical person taps instead of remembering commands, and the bot still receives only commands and replies to its own messages, not the rest of the group conversation | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D65 | A `reboot` by SMS only when fresh and within a daily limit, numbers compared by their end (R46, R46b, R46e) | an SMS delivered hours late, or lying in the store at the start, must not restart the router; a sender number can be forged, hence the restart limit; the network may give the sender in national form; without NTP the age is still checked, because the unsynced clock is never ahead of real time (a late copy is refused), and only the bound towards the future is left out; stated limit: the centre's stamp is read as router-local time (`sms_tool` drops the zone), so an admin number whose SMS centre stamps in another time zone cannot restart by SMS, and `status` is not affected, as it has no age check | 2026-11-09 (from: commit 05891a8fac) |
| D66 | Operator preset chosen by IMSI, `LOW_BALANCE` in the preset's currency, `ROUTER_NAME` in SMS replies (R47, R48) | the generic image must work with any SIM: a balance only where a preset exists, and a new operator is one data file with no code change | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D67 | Joining SMS sent in parts, 10 min for a missing part (R34g) | a long SMS otherwise arrives as several messages with broken sentences, sometimes in reverse order; after 10 min an incomplete one goes out so nothing is lost | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D68 | Commands in SMS replies and in `README.md` in lower case (`status`, `reboot`); the router takes any case (R46a, R46) | upper case suggests that commands must be typed in upper case | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D69 | An SMS reply goes to the number from `SMS_ADMINS` without `+` (R46) | `sms_tool` 2025.08.23 encodes `+` as a digit, so the network accepted replies to `+<number>` that never arrived; a number of 6 or more digits goes out as international anyway, and a national sender form has no country code | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D70 | The project is called `lonewrt`: scripts, paths and variables use the prefix `lw` (`lw-notify`, `/usr/lib/lw/`, `LW_*`), uci package `lonewrt` | a template repository needs a name of its own, and the prefix keeps its files apart from OpenWrt's | 2026-11-08 (from: commit 4c4a6171b2) |
| D71 | The sender field of the SMS list is repaired, and an unreadable list is reported once (R34h, R26f) | `sms_tool` does not escape the sender, one text sender ID with a quote would stop forwarding, SMS control and the cleaning without a trace; skipping the message would need a parser of our own and the owner would not see it, while the repair is two characters in one field that ends at a fixed boundary; what cannot be repaired is reported after 3 reads in a row instead of being a silent state | 2026-11-09 (from: commit 05891a8fac) |
| D72 | A message of the operator's sender is a balance only within 10 min of a query of the router; every matching reply in that window counts (R36) | the sender of an SMS can be forged, and a forged balance would change `/status`, and with an amount above the threshold stay out of the chat; an answer after 10 min is shown as an ordinary SMS, so the owner sees what arrives; "once" was rejected: a forged `Imate: 99.00` between the weekly query and the real low reply would take the slot and hide the low-balance warning, which worked before; accepted leftover: a forged reply after the real one, inside the window, changes what `/status` shows until the next real reply (the warning has already gone out); the first scan after a flash or a reset keeps the old way, because it forwards nothing, there is no stored balance yet, and a forged value there is replaced by the next real reply | 2026-11-09 (from: commit 05891a8fac) |
| D73 | The root password may be given in clear text as `ROOT_PASSWORD`, next to `ROOT_PASSWORD_HASH` (R33b) | owners should not need to know how to make a crypt hash; the profile already holds the WiFi key in clear text and should be 0600 (R3a), and only the hash goes into the image | 2026-11-09 (from: commit 09426ce17a) |
| D74 | WiFi encryption `psk2` (WPA2 only) instead of `psk-mixed` (R7) | the owner's decision of 2026-11-09: WPA1/TKIP is off, and every device of the guests supports WPA2; accepted cost: a very old device that knows only WPA1 does not connect | 2026-11-09 (from: commit 18378d5348) |
| D75 | The first greeting of the bot says the router was reset (R37f; since D84 as a message before the greeting) | a button reset wipes the logs, the SMS archive and the SMS limit counters, and the owner abroad would learn of it only indirectly; the router cannot tell a reset from a flash, both start with empty state, so the lines stand for "fresh state", decided at the first pass from an empty `/etc/lw-notify`, because the keyboard flag alone is also missing after an update with the state kept (`sysupgrade -o`) from an image without the keyboard; a plain `sysupgrade` keeps `/etc/config` but not `/etc/lw-notify`, so it gets the lines too; the lines were first in the greeting itself, because the owner wanted no extra notification, which D84 changed | 2026-11-15 (from: commit 4b9ea9f84f) |
| D76 | A reply pattern with only number characters between `{any}` and a number placeholder is rejected, not repaired (R47c) | the preset is a hand-written data file and the fault would show only as a wrong balance when the first reply comes; a rejection says so in syslog at once, while changing the pattern silently would change what the file says; the rule is on the characters (spaces, digits, dot, comma) and not on the two forms seen, because every such gap lets `{any}` end inside the number | 2026-11-09 (from: commit 18378d5348) |
| D77 | The third-party `.apk` files are checked against `deps.lst` when `build.sh` copies them, only the pinned set is copied, and old versions are removed by exact package name (R2a) | a file in `dl/extra` that was swapped or left over after the download check would otherwise go into an image that carries the profile's secrets | 2026-11-09 (from: commit 09426ce17a) |
| D78 | A profile, or a private key file of its folder, readable by others with a secret in it is a warning, not an error (R3a) | the build is correct either way and a shared machine is the owner's call; the warning costs one `chmod` | 2026-11-09 (from: commit 09426ce17a) |
| D79 | The wait before watchcat restarts `wwan` is a profile key, `LINK_RESTART_AFTER_S`, a whole number from 120 to 900 with the default 120 (R14, R14a) | A short wait reduces outage time while a longer wait avoids restarting on transient loss. The upper bound keeps interface repair ahead of the router restart (interface timing, R14a; original timing evidence: commit `f9ca2f8`, `SPEC.md`, this decision). | 2026-11-11 (from: commit e71fc8d7fc) |
| D80 | In-place restore of the data session by polling, in `lw-linkwatch`, with `ifup wwan` only after 20 s (R49, R49a, R49b, R49c, R49d, R49e, R49f, R49g, R12c) | The modem can register again while the QMI data call remains dead and netifd still shows the interface up. Polling the existing client restores service sooner than an interface restart and avoids a second modem reader (restoration and escalation, R49c, R49d; original modem evidence: commit `f9ca2f8`, `SPEC.md`, this decision). | 2026-11-11 (from: commit e71fc8d7fc) |
| D81 | A profile key `LOG_LEVEL`, `info` or `debug`, in the image as uci `lonewrt.log.level` (R3, R29c) | the owner wants to run an image with detailed, persistent logs of the modem link for a test period and read from them how the watcher behaves on the live router, while the usual image stays light on flash (Risks). A build key gives the same result after a flash and after a reset (D2), and one level for all scripts keeps one place to look; the `debug` level has its own syslog priority so the lines can be told apart and filtered. The debug log is 2 MB because the extra lines (signal samples, steps, heartbeat) would otherwise push the `.old` history out in a few days. Only the signal sample and the watcher use it: a longer log is for the new code, and the logs of the older scripts, which the tests and the owner know, stay as they are | 2026-11-11 (from: commit 5af53c522b) |
| D82 | The image's `qmi.sh` is the `uqmi` package's file without its `--network-scan` line, the mode set stays (`network.wwan.modes=lte`), and `network.wwan.timeout` is 30 (R12d, R12f, R7, R10). See signal logging, interface restart timing and Auto startup comparison (R28, R14a, R73i) | The network scan adds time to every start, while keeping the mode set holds LTE-only after registration. A longer registration limit preserves the time the scan used to allow and the build guard avoids silently overriding a changed upstream handler (QMI derivation and pin, R12d, R12e; original start measurements: commit `f9ca2f8`, `SPEC.md`, this decision). | 2026-11-23 (from: commit 3d3d1a6041) |
| D83 | The bot sends HTML (`parse_mode=HTML`) with the tags `b`, `i`, `code` and `blockquote` (R37g, R43) | a guest can copy the Wi-Fi password with one tap on a `<code>` span instead of selecting it, and a quote sets the operator's or sender's own words apart from the router's, so a message reads at a glance; MarkdownV2 was rejected because it needs far more characters escaped (`.`, `-`, `(`, `!` and others), and text from an SMS holds all of them; HTML needs only `&`, `<` and `>`, which one function covers | 2026-11-15 (from: commit 4b9ea9f84f) |
| D84 | The reset note is a message of its own before the greeting, not lines in it (R37f) | a tap on the keyboard of a group arrives as a reply to the message that carries the keyboard, and the app quotes the start of that message above every tap: with the note in the greeting, every tap in the chat read `Router was reset`; the greeting with the keyboard now starts with its own words. It costs one more message once, at the first start. This replaces the "one message" of the second-LAN decision (D75) | 2026-11-15 (from: commit 4b9ea9f84f) |
| D85 | A message Telegram rejects as HTML goes once more as plain text and is logged (R37g, R34f) | the markup is ours, and a mistake in it, for example a text that escaping missed, must not cost a message that carries an SMS or a warning; the plain copy has the same words without the tags, and the log line says that a markup fault exists, with no text in it, so it gets fixed (an edit has no such copy: a 400 also answers an edit that changes nothing, a double tap, and a plain copy would replace the formatted text) | 2026-11-15 (from: commit 4b9ea9f84f) |
| D86 | The answer to `/balance` edits the acknowledgement into the balance message (R36, R37) | the owner asks and waits: one message that turns from "asked" into the result is shorter than two, and the chat shows no stale "asked" line; the edit makes no sound, which is why a late reply, the weekly reply and the first amount below the threshold come as new messages | 2026-11-15 (from: commit 4b9ea9f84f) |
| D87 | The prompt for a device name is a reply request (`force_reply`) and the name is read from the reply (R50) | the bot runs with privacy mode on in the group (D64), so it sees only commands and replies to its own messages; a plain message with the name would never reach it, and a reply to the prompt does. The app opens the reply field by itself, so nobody has to find the reply action | 2026-11-15 (from: commit ddc7e4dfe2) |
| D88 | The names of devices are one file on flash, written when a name changes (R50a) | the owner's decision. A name typed by hand is worth more than a DHCP name and must survive a restart and a power cut, which the DHCP names do not promise (they go to flash once a day, D63); a person types a name rarely, so the wear is nothing. Like everything else in `/etc/lw-notify` it is lost on a new flash and a button reset (R37f) | 2026-11-15 (from: commit ddc7e4dfe2) |
| D89 | The limits of naming: 12 devices and a Cancel in the list, 24 characters, 64 names, 10 min, short names two to a row (R50, R50a) | 12 buttons and a Cancel fit a phone screen without scrolling; 24 characters fit a line of `/devices`; 64 names are more than a home has devices and bound the file; 10 min is long enough to type a name and short enough that a forgotten list does not stay live (all of it is in RAM, so a restart ends it); two short names in a row halve the height of the list | 2026-11-15 (from: commit ddc7e4dfe2) |
| D90 | `/wifi` answers with a photo of a QR code and a self-contained caption (R51, R43) | a guest joins by pointing the phone camera at the code, with no typing of a password, and the owner can forward the message as it is to someone outside the group, so the caption holds no command and no "for guests"; the code is the one of the first network because a message carries one picture and the first is the one `/wifi` lists first; the caption says `Password: none` where the text of the device list rule (R43) says `No password`, because the caption is read away from the other lines of the bot | 2026-11-15 (from: commit 87e3059525) |
| D91 | The picture is made with the stock `qrencode` (text output), an awk filter and `cjpeg`, as a JPEG; packages `qrencode` (20 KiB installed, with `libqrencode` 28 KiB) and `libjpeg-turbo-utils` (136 KiB, with `libjpeg-turbo` 356 KiB), 540 KiB in all (R51, R2) | the `qrencode` package of OpenWrt 25.12.6 is built without libpng: `-t PNG` prints "PNG output is disabled at compile time" and writes no file, so no PNG comes from the feed packages; a PNG with `libpng` (156 KiB) would need our own build of `qrencode` that we would then carry and pin; `cjpeg` reads the plain-text PGM of the awk filter as it is (measured with libjpeg-turbo 3.1.1 in the x86 rootfs, and the JPEGs of quality 90, 8 to 12 pixels per module, read back with `zbarimg` as the same text), and Telegram takes a JPEG as a photo; the 540 KiB installed are about 6 % of the 8.9 MB image (the image with them is not built yet); the pipe has no file of a megabyte in RAM, and the picture is made again for every request, so nothing stays in RAM or on flash | 2026-11-15 (from: commit 87e3059525) |
| D92 | No picture means the text of the device list rule (R43) and one log line, not silence or an error message (R51) | the owner asks for the password to give it to a person standing there, so an answer with the password is worth more than a picture: a tool that is missing, fails or writes nothing, a caption over the limit, or a refusal of the photo (HTTP 400) all end in the text; the log line says why in ASCII, with no name, no password and no token, and a network failure is not answered with a second try, as for every command (R34c) | 2026-11-15 (from: commit 87e3059525) |
| D93 | The start of the month's counters after a reset is the boot after it, noted once in `/etc/lw-notify` (R44c) | after the flash of 15 Nov the chat showed `0.1 GB this month (since 1 Nov)` while the counters ran from 12:53 that day; `nlbw` gives no start time, and `/etc/nlbwmon` and `/etc/lw-notify` are wiped and kept by the same resets (neither is in the sysupgrade keep list), so a missing mark means fresh counters; the boot time (synced clock minus uptime) is when nlbwmon started counting | 2026-11-15 (from: commit a0061274ce) |
| D94 | The release version lives in one file, `VERSION`, read by the build; it is not part of the `src/` hash and it is the first line of `/etc/lw-build` (R3b, R7b) | a release is one edit of one file plus the tag `v<version>` (`docs/development.md`), and the image can say which release it is without a `git` checkout on the build host. The hash of `src/` stays a hash of the sources, so a version bump alone does not look like a code change; readers that grep `src_sha256=` see the line unchanged. A malformed value stops the build instead of shipping an image with a version nobody can compare (SemVer 2.0.0 without build metadata, which the image has no use for) | 2026-11-15 (from: commit 50cda1289c) |
| D95 | One profile is the only input of every way to build (R52) | (not recorded) | 2026-11-16 (from: commit 240d7b8649) |

One preset per profile, because a router has one SIM. The build runs `operators.sh` instead of repeating its rules in `render.py`, so there is one set of rules; the preview has to repeat them in JavaScript, and a test compares its answers with the router's code.
